1. Project Overview
   Project Name
   MenuListAi

One-Line Definition
MenuListAi is an autonomous decision system that runs daily menu-driven decisions for offline SMB businesses across customer-facing, in-store, human, and marketing surfaces — without dashboards, explanations, or configuration burden.

Core Philosophy
Authority over transparency.
MenuList does not explain decisions. It behaves consistently enough to be followed.

MenuList is not:

A restaurant management system

A marketing tool

An analytics dashboard

A content generator

MenuList decides, then executes quietly across surfaces.

2. Vision & Mission
   Vision
   To become the default operating intelligence layer for offline SMB businesses — where decisions happen automatically, silently, and reliably every day.

Mission
To eliminate daily decision fatigue for SMB owners by:

Observing customer behavior passively

Applying strict confidence thresholds

Acting only when certainty is high

Remaining silent when certainty is low

3. Target Market
   Primary Market
   Offline SMB businesses, specifically:

Restaurants

Cafes

Cloud kitchens

Salons & spas (menu-style service catalogs)

Market Characteristics (Observed, Not Assumed)
Owner-operated or owner-led

Staff behavior mirrors owner behavior

Low tolerance for experimentation

High sensitivity to embarrassment

Strong preference for obvious, repeatable actions

Limited appetite for dashboards or analytics

Geographic Focus
Primary: India (WhatsApp-first, offline-heavy)

Secondary: Global SMBs (US, UK, EU) with adaptation via execution surfaces

4. Core Value Proposition
   For Owners
   “MenuList tells me what to do today — and I don’t have to think about it.”

For Customers
Faster decisions, fewer questions, clearer choices.

For Staff
Reduced ambiguity in what to recommend.

The Real Value
MenuList removes decisions, not adds features.

5. Key Differentiators (Non-Negotiable)
   5.1 Decision-Centric, Not Feature-Centric
   MenuList is built around daily decisions, not tools.

Every surface answers one question only:

“What should happen today?”

5.2 Confidence-Gated Automation
Actions occur only when confidence thresholds are met.

Surface Confidence Threshold
Campaigns / Social ≥ 0.6
Digital Screens ≥ 0.7
Physical Surfaces ≥ 0.7–0.8
Staff Prompt ≥ 0.8
Silence is a valid and intentional outcome.

5.3 Authority Without Explanation
No “why this worked”

No percentages on decision surfaces

No optimization language

No experimentation signals

MenuList behaves like infrastructure, not software.

5.4 Unified Multi-Surface Execution
One intelligence loop powers:

Menu decision blocks

Digital screens

Printed physical surfaces

Staff speech

Social amplification

All derive from the same nightly intelligence loop.

6. Core System Components (High-Level)
   MenuList is composed of six locked production features:

Continuous Menu Intelligence (CMI)
Nightly behavioral analysis engine.

Decision Blocks
Customer-facing menu nudges (Popular, Quick Pick, Best Value).

Digital Screens
Public, high-visibility authority surfaces.

Physical Surfaces
Printed artifacts (tent cards, stickers) — irreversible trust surfaces.

Staff Prompt Mode
Human speech standardization via owner mediation.

Social Content (Today Surface)
Campaign-driven amplification via passive & active campaigns.

Each feature is fully implemented, verified, and frozen.

7. Success Metrics (Internal Only)
   MenuList does not expose success metrics on decision surfaces.

Internal KPIs:

Metric Purpose
Feature activation rate Adoption health
Campaign export count Execution signal
Silence rate Trust protection
Skip rate Owner confidence calibration
Firebase cost/store Unit economics
Support “why” tickets Authority leakage detection 8. Timeline & Milestones

Strict cost governance

No roadmap drift allowed

F-01 — CONTINUOUS MENU INTELLIGENCE (CMI)
Feature ID
F-01

Business Objective
To silently observe customer interaction behavior and compute confidence-scored item intelligence without exposing analytics or explanations.

CMI is the only intelligence source in MenuListAi.

Target User / Persona
Primary: System (autonomous)

Secondary: None (owners never see CMI directly)

Detailed Workflow
Data Ingestion (Passive)

Menu views

Item taps

Decision block interactions

QR scans

Time-of-day context

Nightly Intelligence Loop

Runs once daily (2:30 AM UTC)

Aggregates last N days (sliding window)

Applies decay to older signals

Scoring

Calculates confidence score ∈ [0,1]

Stability tracked via consecutive-day confidence

Output

Writes results into CampaignsSummaryDocument

No runtime scoring allowed

Core Logic (High-Level)
confidence(item) =
availabilityScore ×
behaviorScore ×
stabilityScore ×
timingScore
All sub-scores ∈ [0,1]

Multiplicative → weak signals collapse confidence

1. ARCHITECTURE PRINCIPLES (NON-NEGOTIABLE)
   MenuListAi architecture is governed by authority-first system design, not transparency or configurability.

Core Principles
Frontend-First Architecture

Intelligence is precomputed

Frontend only renders decisions

No runtime AI or scoring

Silence Is a Feature

No data = no UI

Suppression is intentional, not failure

Single Source of Truth (SSOT)

All features read from the same summary

↓
Decision Blocks (0.65)
↓

↓
Physical Surfaces (0.7–0.8)
↓

Campaign Type System
export type CampaignKind = "active" | "passive";

export type CampaignType =
// Active
| "meal_push"
| "bestseller_boost"
| "slow_item_rescue"
| "festival"
| "new_item"
// Passive
| "todays_special"
| "weekend_pick"
| "now_available"
| "menu_highlight";
Campaign Document (Firestore)
campaigns/{tId}/{sId}/{campaignId}
interface CampaignDocument {
id: string;
tId: string;
sId: string;
projectId: string;

kind: CampaignKind;
type: CampaignType;

subject: {
itemId?: string;
itemName?: string;
};

intent: OutputIntent;
primarySurface: ExecutionSurface;
secondarySurfaces: ExecutionSurface[];

status: "suggested" | "completed" | "skipped" | "suppressed";

confidence: {
availabilityScore: number;
behaviorScore: number;
timingScore: number;
total: number;
};

suggestedFor: string; // YYYY-MM-DD
createdOn: Timestamp;
resolvedOn?: Timestamp;

assets?: {
imageUrl?: string;
caption?: string;
whatsappMessage?: string;
posterPdfUrl?: string;
source: "existing_image" | "generated_image";
};

outcome?: {
signal: "positive" | "neutral" | "insufficient_data";
observation: string;
closure: string;
};

skipCount: number;
suppressedUntil?: Timestamp;
}
Summary Document (SSOT)
platformSummary/campaigns\_{sId}
interface CampaignsSummaryDocument {
lastUpdated: Timestamp;

today: {
primary?: TodayCampaignSummary;
operational: TodayCampaignSummary[];
isEmpty: boolean;
};

stats: {
totalCompleted: number;
totalSkipped: number;
typeSkipCounts: Record<CampaignType, number>;
};
} 5. CAMPAIGN ENGINE (DETERMINISTIC FLOW)
Engine Entry Point
generateTodayCampaigns(storeContext)
Execution Order (STRICT)
Fetch menu + availability

Compute signals

Calculate confidence

Apply suppression rules

Select ONE primary

Select max TWO operational

Write summary document

No retries. No ranking UI.

Suppression Logic
if (skipCount >= 2) {
suppressFor(7 days)
}
Suppression is per campaign type, not global.

   SEV-3 Data integrity risk Immediate freeze
   Incident Flow (MANDATORY)
   Detect → Isolate → Disable → Observe → Fix → Re-enable
   Never fix live.
   Always disable first.

Feature Flag Kill Switches
Flag Scope
SOCIAL_CONTENT_ENABLED Entire Today system
SOCIAL_CONTENT_IMAGE_GENERATION Image pipeline only
DIGITAL_SCREENS_ENABLED Screen rendering
STAFF_PROMPT_ENABLED Staff prompts
Flags live in src/config/features.ts.

6. BACKUP & DISASTER RECOVERY
   Data Safety
   Data Strategy
   Firestore Google-managed redundancy
   Storage Regional replication
   Config Git versioned
   Feature flags Source-controlled
   Recovery Scenarios
   Scenario Response
   Corrupt summary doc Regenerate nightly
   Campaign doc issue Mark suppressed
   Export log spike No action
   No manual backfills unless data loss confirmed.

7. USER SUPPORT WORKFLOWS
   Tiered Support Model
   Tier Who Scope
   L1 Support UI, usage
   L2 Ops Feature flags
   L3 Engineering Code only

   “Nothing showing today” “That’s expected sometimes.”
   “Why this item?” “It’s prepared based on customer behavior.”
   “Can I change it?” “You can skip it.”
   Forbidden Support Behavior
   ❌ Explaining logic
   ❌ Showing confidence values
   ❌ Promising future behavior
   ❌ Debugging in front of users

8. FEATURE FLAG MANAGEMENT
   Rules

No gradual sliders

No per-user overrides

Tenant-level only

Monitor 48 hours
